# N(P, 2p-2) for labeled trees on p labeled vertices, one pattern from each reversal pair.
# N(P, t) counts labeled trees on t vertices with no ordered minor isomorphic to P.
# The reversal of P replaces every label i by p+1-i and has the same N at every t, so it is omitted.
# Each kept Prüfer code is the lexicographically smaller of the two codes in its reversal pair.
# Patterns are grouped by unlabeled isomorphism class.
# For every p from 3 through 7 the list is complete, and no count is shared by two isomorphism classes.
# Two patterns from distinct isomorphism classes therefore differ at t = 2p-2, so they are not Wilf equivalent.
# The p=8 section is the partial set of t=14 counts computed so far, still with reversals removed.

# p=3 t=4  reversal-pairs listed=2 of 2  isomorphism-classes=1  shared-counts-across-classes=0

N([2], 4) = 2
